LP step details:

Detailed mirror descent algorithm:
```
def _refine_mirror(y, end_time, seed, tau_start=0.25, tau_end=0.01, lr=0.05):
    n=y.size
    a=softmax(y); best=a; best_score=score(a)
    m=zeros(n); v=zeros(n); t=0
    tau = tau_start * max(c)
    lr_cur=lr
    no_improve=0
    while time < end_time:
       a=softmax(y)
       c=conv(a)
       score=...
       if score < best_score: update best, no_improve=0, maybe lr*1.02
       else no_improve++
       # fail safe: if no_improve > threshold, perturb y or reset to best
       cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<=0: break
       # dynamic temperature: warmup broad then narrow based on time progress
       frac = elapsed/total
       target_tau = (tau_start + (tau_end - tau_start)*frac) * cmax
       # anneal gradually: tau = min? maybe use target
       w = softmax((c-cmax)/target_tau)  # weights sum 1
       h = _conv_a_w(a,w)
       ga = h + a*(w[2i])  # w length 2n-1, 2i valid; for large okay
       ga = clean
       dot = dot(a,ga)
       gy = a*(ga - dot)  # gradient wrt y
       gy = clean
       norm = sqrt(mean(gy*gy)) + eps
       step = lr_cur * gy / norm
       # Adam
       t+=1; m=...; v=...; alpha=1e-2? Use updated lr maybe 0.1
       y_new = y - step
       y_new -= max(y_new)
       # line search: try y_new; if score not improved and no_improve? maybe try half
       a_new=softmax(y_new); score_new=score(a_new)
       if score_new < score - 1e-12:
           y = y_new; a=a_new; score=score_new; lr_cur=min(lr_max, lr_cur*1.03)
       else:
           # try 0.5
           y_half = y - 0.5*step
           a_half=softmax; sc_half=score
           if sc_half < score:
              y=y_half; ...; lr_cur unchanged?
           else:
              lr_cur=max(lr_min, lr_cur*0.85)
              no_improve++
              if no_improve>30:
                 # restart from best with random perturbation
                 y = log(best) + rng.normal(0,0.05); center
                 m=v=0; no_improve=0; lr_cur=lr
    return best, best_score
```

`_pow2(m)`:
```
def _pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`_clean(v)`:
```
def _clean(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    return np.maximum(v,0.0)
```

`_normalize(v)`:
```
def _normalize(v):
   v=_clean(v)
   s=v.sum()
   if not finite or s<1e-300: return zeros? uniform
   return v/s
```
For softmax, we want not uniform if zero; but fallback.

`_project_simplex(v)` maybe not needed if using softmax, but for local random and candidates. We can implement as previous or simple normalize after max0. For random multiplicative, normalize enough (no lower bound). We often need nonnegative; max0 then normalize. No need exact simplex projection for add/sub. Use `_normalize`.

`_fft_conv(a, L)`:
```
def _fft_conv(a, L):
    n=a.size
    if L is None: L=_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    C=A*A
    c=np.fft.irfft(C, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size <= 1: return c
    # remove tiny negative
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
```
Sum c may be scaled; if L too small? L>=2n-1 ok. For n=0? handle.

`_score_from_c(c,n)`: return 2*n*max(c). If max <0 set0.

`_score(a, L=None)`:
```
a=_normalize(a) # maybe not if already normalized, but okay; it clips negatives.
c=_fft_conv(a,L)
return 2.0*a.size*max(c)
```
But for many score calls, re-normalizing sum may add cost. We can have `_score_norm(a)` assuming normalized. For candidates, normalize. In loops, a normalized. Use `_score_c` maybe.

`_score_and_c(a,L)`: returns score,c. Normalize? no.

`_softmax_y(y)`:
```
def _softmax_y(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64)
   y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -1000.0)
   y=y - np.max(y)
   y=np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(y.size,1/n)
   return e/s
```
For n=1, returns [1].

`_log_of_a(a)`:
```
a=_clean(a); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 uniform
a=a/s
eps = 1e-12 / n ?
y=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
y = y - y.max()
return np.clip(y,-100,100)
```
If a has zeros (e.g., parent), log(-). Use small epsilon relative max. `eps=a.max()*1e-12` or 1e-300. Fine.

`_resample(a0, n)`:
```
a0=_clean(a0)
m=a0.size
if m==n: return _normalize(a0)
if m==0: uniform
# log interpolation
y0=np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
y_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,y0)
a=_softmax_y(y_new)
return a
```
Interpolate log; if zeros, -690. Good.

`_param_sequence(n, p=0.5, q=0.0, alpha=1.0, beta=1.0, w_right=0.0, w_uniform=0.0, slope=0.0, quad=0.0)`
```
i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
t=i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0
v=np.power(i+alpha, -p)
if w_uniform: v += w_uniform
if abs(slope)>...: v *= np.exp(slope*t + quad*t*t)
if w_right !=0:
   v += w_right*np.power(n-1-i+beta, -q)
return _normalize(v)
```
If p<0? Use negative exponent? We'll keep p≥0. If q=0, np.power(..., -0)=1.

Maybe one-sided with exponent p can be 0.6 but continuous bad; discrete offset handles. Include p values.

`_subgradient_refine(y, end_time, seed, verbose=False)`:
Need precompute Lscore, Lgrad in function.
Parameters: start_lr=0.03, min_lr=1e-5, max_lr=0.2; tau_start=0.25, tau_end=0.005; beta1=0.9,beta2=0.99.

Pseudo:
```
def _subgradient_refine(y, end_time, seed):
    y = np.asarray(y, float); y = clean; n=y.size
    Lscore=_pow2(2*n-1); Lgrad=_pow2(3*n-2)
    a=_softmax_y(y); c=_fft_conv(a,Lscore); sc=...
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=1e-2; beta1=0.9; beta2=0.99
    start=time.monotonic(); total=max(1,end_time-start); last_best=start; no=0
    # maybe initial small warm-up: use gradient to move? 
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       # if no improvement for 30s, restart best perturbed
       now=time.monotonic()
       frac=min(1,(now-start)/total)
       tau = (0.25*(1-frac)+0.005*frac)
       cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<=0: break
       # weights
       z=(c - cmax)/ (tau*cmax)
       z=np.clip(z, -745, 0)
       e=np.exp(z); w=e/e.sum()
       h=_conv_a_w(a,w,Lgrad)
       # diag: idx=2*np.arange(n); wdiag=w[idx]
       ga = h + a*np.take(w, 2*np.arange(n))
       ga = _clean? ga can tiny negative? Since w,a nonneg, h nonneg. Use np.maximum(ga,0)
       dot = float(np.dot(a,ga))
       gy = a*(ga-dot)
       gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
       # center? gy sum ~0; maybe gy -= mean? No, multiplicative.
       # Adam
       t+=1
       m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*gy
       v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(gy*gy)
       mh=m/(1-beta1**t)
       vh=v/(1-beta2**t)
       step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
       step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
       # Bound step to avoid huge
       max_step = 0.25
       step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
       y_new = y - step
       y_new = np.where(np.isfinite(y_new), y_new, 0.0)
       y_new -= np.max(y_new)
       a_new=_softmax_y(y_new)
       c_new=_fft_conv(a_new,Lscore)
       sc_new=...
       soft_cur = ... from c,w? We haven't computed soft_sum? We have e sum. soft = cmax + tau*cmax? Wait if z=(c-cmax)/(tau*cmax), soft = cmax + (tau*cmax)*log(sume)? Because lse with temperature T=tau*cmax. If w=e/sum, soft = cmax + T log sume. Compute T=tau*cmax.
       soft_new = cmax_new + T_new? Need compute after. We can compute in helper.
       if sc_new < sc - 1e-13 or (soft improved and sc_new <= sc+1e-9): accept
       else: try half step: y_half = y - 0.5 step; ... if accept; else reduce lr
       if accept:
           y=y_new; a=a_new;c=c_new;sc=sc_new
           if sc<best_sc: best=a_new; best_sc=sc; last_best=now; no=0; lr=min(max, lr*1.02)
           else no +=1? If exact not best but improving from current due to soft? Could still no best. Set no no if exact improved? Use exact best. 
       else:
           no +=1
           lr=max(min, lr*0.9)
           if no>60 or now-last_best>30:
              # perturb
              y = _log_of_a(best) + rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
              y = y - y.max()
              m.fill(0);v.fill(0);t=0
              a=_softmax_y(y); c=_fft_conv; sc=...
              if sc>best_sc: y=log(best) maybe; sc=best
              no=0; last_best=now; lr=1e-2
       # print occasionally
    return best, best_sc
```
Need compute soft curve. We can write helper `_soft_value_c(c, tau)` returning cmax + T log sum exp((c-cmax)/T). But in loop we already compute e. T=tau*cmax. `soft_cur = cmax + T*np.log(e.sum())` (e sum). After y_new, we can compute c_new and T_new for soft_new with same tau? Use current tau for comparing? ```

`_conv_a_w(a, w, L)`:
```
def _conv_a_w(a,w,L):
    n=a.size
    r=np.flip(w)
    Ah=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    Wh=np.fft.rfft(r, n=L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Ah*Wh, n=L)[:3*n-2]
    # h[i] for i 0..n-1: conv[2n-2-i]
    idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
    h = conv[idx]
    return np.maximum(h,0.0)
```
Check L needs ≥ n + len(r) -1 = 3n-2. Use L>=pow2. For n=1, len r=1, L=1. Good.
Could w have sum 1; r sum1. For large n, h nonnegative. Numerical negative clip.

`_gradient_soft` could be used by L-BFGS too. We'll implement separate `_soft_gradient(y, tau)`? But for mirror, inline.

Local search:
```
def _local_search(a0, end_time, seed):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; L=pow2(2n-1)
    best=a; best_sc=score
    rng=...
    sigma=0.15
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    while time<end_time:
       cand=best.copy()
       nblocks=1+int(rng.integers(0,3))
       for _ in range(nblocks):
          w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(log2, log(n//4))))
          s = int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
          cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
       cand=_normalize(cand)
       sc=score
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best=cand; last_best=now; sigma=min(0.4,sigma*1.03)
       else:
           if now-last_best>5: sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.8)
           if now-last_best>20 and now-start>30:
               # global perturb
               cand=best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05)); if score<best accept
    return best,best_sc
```
But local search O(FFT) per trial; maybe less efficient than gradient. Keep short.

   else: q=rng.uniform(0.05,0.55); wr=np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(0.02), np.log(5.0))); beta=rng.uniform(0.5,2.5)

   wu= rng.uniform(0,0.2) if rng.random()<0.5 else 0.0
   slope = rng.uniform(-1,1) if rng.random()<0.3 else 0.0
   return p,q,alpha,beta,wr,wu,slope
```

The problem can be seen as approximate square root of a polynomial with coefficients bounded. We can try to solve the recurrence for first n coefficients y_j to make tail flat. Since P length n, if we choose y_j (first n) and derive a_j, the tail coefficients are determined. We can optimize y_j as a smooth profile to minimize max(y_j, tail). This is a different parameterization that directly controls first half. Perhaps better than arbitrary a. We can implement a low-dimensional y-profile and use recurrence O(n^2)? For n=8192, O(n^2) too slow. But we can compute a via polynomial square root using FFT Newton in O(n log n). Let's examine.

Given y_j for j=0..n-1 (desired c_j), solve P^2 = Y + x^n R (higher unknown). There is a known divide-and-conquer Newton to compute square root of a power series up to n-1 in O(n log n). But implementing is complex. However, if y_j = constant * f(j/n) maybe can use recurrence O(n * K) if f has short basis? For general no.

Could use direct iterative solution: Starting with a guess a, update a based on y? Since y are first square coefficients. We can choose y profile and compute a by solving nonlinear system. Maybe use fixed point: a_j = sqrt? Not.

Now, full subgradient refine details with adaptive tau. Let's code carefully.

```
def _subgradient_refine(y, end_time, seed):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    y = _clean? y can negative. Use finite.
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)  # but may flatten; okay
    n = y.size
    if n < 2: return softmax, 2? 
    Lscore = _pow2(2*n-1); Lgrad = _pow2(3*n-2)
    a = _softmax_y(y)
    c = _fft_conv(a, Lscore)
    sc = _score_c(c,n)
    best = a.copy(); best_sc = sc
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=1; b1=0.9; b2=0.99; p1=1.0; p2=1.0
    lr=0.02; lr_min=1e-5; lr_max=0.25
    tau_rel=0.25
    t_start=time.monotonic(); total=max(1e-6, end_time - t_start)
    last_best=t_start; last_print=t_start
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       now=time.monotonic()
       # If current a not normalized due to softmax? yes.
       cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       if cmax <= 1e-300: break
       T = tau_rel * cmax
       if T <= 1e-300: T=1e-300
       z = (c - cmax)/T; z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
       e = np.exp(z); s = e.sum();
       if s <=0 or not finite: w = np.full(2n-1,1/(2n-1)); s=...
       else w = e/s
       soft_cur = cmax + T * np.log(s)
       h = _conv_a_w(a, w, Lgrad)
       idx = 2*np.arange(n)
       ga = h + a * np.take(w, idx)
       ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
       dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
       gy = a * (ga - dot)
       gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
       # if gy all zero -> no update
       t += 1
       p1 *= b1; p2 *= b2
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gy
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(gy*gy)
       mh = m/(1-p1) if 1-p1>1e-12 else m
       vh = v/(1-p2) if 1-p2>1e-12 else v
       step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
       step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
       step = np.clip(step, -0.25, 0.25)
       # Try full and half
       accepted=False; y_accepted=None; ...
       for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           y_new = y - scale*step
           y_new = np.where(np.isfinite(y_new), y_new, 0.0)
           y_new -= np.max(y_new)
           y_new = np.clip(y_new, -80.0, 0.0)
           a_new = _softmax_y(y_new)
           c_new = _fft_conv(a_new, Lscore)
           sc_new = _score_c(c_new,n)
           # soft_new with same T
           cnx=max(c_new); zx=(c_new-cnx)/T; ex=exp; sx=sum; soft_new=cnx+T log(sx)
           if (sc_new < sc - 1e-13) or (soft_new < soft_cur - 1e-12 and sc_new <= sc + 1e-9):
               y=y_new; a=a_new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new; scale_used=scale; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if sc < best_sc:
               best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; last_best=now; no=0; lr=min(lr_max,lr*1.02); tau_rel=max(0.005,tau_rel*0.97)
           else:
               no += 1
               tau_rel=max(0.005,tau_rel*0.98)
       else:
           no +=1
           lr=max(lr_min,lr*0.9)
           tau_rel=min(0.8,tau_rel*1.1)
       # restart if stuck
       if no > 50 or (now - last_best > 45.0):
           # return to best and perturb
           y = _log_of_a(best)
           y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -20.0)
           y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
           y -= np.max(y)
           y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)
           a=_softmax_y(y); c=_fft_conv(a,Lscore); sc=_score_c(c,n)
           m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=1; p1=1.0; p2=1.0
           no=0; last_best=now; lr=0.02; tau_rel=0.3
       if now - last_print > 30:
           print(f"sg t={now-t_start:.1f} score={sc:.8f} best={best_sc:.8f} tau={tau_rel:.4f} lr={lr:.5f} n={n}")
           last_print=now
    return best, best_sc
```
Need note: `np.max(y_new)` if y_new all -inf? finite. Center and clip. But clipping to -80,0 may reduce dynamic range; if y has spread >80, small components underflow to 1e-35; okay. Starting parent may have spread >80? Maybe not. To preserve, could clip to -700,700; but y centered max0, min -2000 would underflow. Use -700? `np.exp(-700)` ~1e-304; okay. But softmax with many -700 may sum small; sum still dominated. Use -700,0. For stability in L-BFGS, bounds 80. For subgradient, use -100? Let's use -80 to avoid underflow min ~1e-35, enough. It may lose zeros but ok. If sequence has a_i below 1e-35 relative max, irrelevant. Use `_clip_log(y, -80, 80)`? Center max0, upper 0. Good.
For parent with some zeros, `_log_of_a` returns -12? if epsilon. okay.

Parameter sequence details:
```
def _param_sequence(n, p, q, alpha, beta, w_right, w_uniform, slope):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.power(i + alpha, -float(p))
    if w_uniform: v += w_uniform
    if slope:
       t = i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0
       v *= np.exp(slope*t)
    if w_right:
       r = np.power(n - 1 - i + beta, -float(q))
       v += w_right * r
    return _normalize(v)
```
If p=0, power 0 =1. If q=0 and w_right, right constant. If q<0? not. Use clip p [0,1], q [0,1], alpha,beta positive.

Random param function:
```
def _random_param(rng):
   p=rng.uniform(0.30,0.70)
   if rng.random()<0.45:
      q=0.0; wr=0.0; beta=1.0
   else:
      q=rng.uniform(0.05,0.55)
      wr=float(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(0.01), np.log(5.0))))
      beta=rng.uniform(0.3,2.5)
   alpha=rng.uniform(0.2,3.0)
   wu=0.0
   if rng.random()<0.3: wu=rng.uniform(0.0,0.5)
   slope=0.0
   if rng.random()<0.25: slope=rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)
   return (p,q,alpha,beta,wr,wu,slope)
```
Maybe slope positive increases right end; negative decreases. Good.

Search for nonnegative sequence minimizing ... using block-constrained L-BFGS macro optimization and full-order multiplicative smoothed-gradient search with FFT.

In `_fft_conv`, if n=0 return empty. If a sum maybe unnormalized. It returns c of input values. For candidate score with unnormalized, denominator handled. For normalized, okay.
```
def _fft_conv(a, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if L is None: L=_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    C=A*A
    c=np.fft.irfft(C,n=L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
If a has negative? candidate nonneg. If unnormalized large, c large but finite. okay.

`_score_norm`:
```
def _score_norm(a,L=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not finite or s<=1e-300: return inf
   c=_fft_conv(a,L)
   mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
   if not finite: return inf
   return 2*n*mx/(s*s)
```
If a normalized, s=1.

```
def _block_sequence_from_y(yB, n, sizes):
    eB=_softmax_y(yB)
    mass=eB*np.asarray(sizes,float)
    s=mass.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    vals=eB/s
    a=np.repeat(vals, sizes)
    # due to sizes int, a size sum sizes=n
    return a
```
If sizes is list of np.int64, repeat ok. `_softmax_y` expects 1D. If B large, okay.

`_make_block_arrays(n,B)`:
```
def _make_block_arrays(n,B):
   B=min(max(1,int(B)), int(n))
   sizes=np.full(B, n//B, dtype=np.int64)
   rem=n % B
   if rem: sizes[:rem] += 1
   offsets=np.empty(B, dtype=np.int64)
   offsets[0]=0
   if B>1: offsets[1:]=np.cumsum(sizes)[:-1]
   return B,sizes,offsets
```
If n=0? not.

def _block_soft_grad(x, n, T, L, sizes, offsets):
   a=_block_sequence_from_y(x,n,sizes)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
   C=A*A
   c=np.fft.irfft(C,n=L)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   f=_soft_value_T(c,T)
   mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
   if mx<=0 or not finite: return f, np.zeros(len(offsets))
   z=(c-mx)/T; z=np.clip(z,-745,0); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.full(2*n-1,1/(2*n-1)); s=2*n-1
   else: w=e/s
   W=np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w),n=L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,n=L)
   length=3*n-2
   if conv.size < length: # pad (shouldn't)
       tmp=np.zeros(length); tmp[:conv.size]=conv; conv=tmp
   else: conv=conv[:length]
   idx=2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
   h=conv[idx]
   ga=h + a*np.take(w, 2*np.arange(n))
   ga=np.where(np.isfinite(ga),ga,0.0)
   dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
   gy=a*(ga-dot)
   gy=np.where(np.isfinite(gy),gy,0.0)
   if len(offsets)==1: gB=gy.sum()
   else: gB=np.add.reduceat(gy, offsets)
   gB=np.asarray(gB,float)
   gB=gB - gB.sum()/len(gB) ? Should we center? For gradient wrt yB, no need? The mapping yB to full y repeat; softmax invariant to adding constant to yB. The gradient of F wrt yB under softmax should sum zero (like full gy sum zero). `gy` sum zero approximately, so `gB` sum zero. Due to numeric, we can center to help L-BFGS: `gB -= gB.mean()`. Is that correct? Since adding constant to yB doesn't change a, objective constant; true gradient should be gauge-variant; centering enforces. Good.
   gB = np.where(np.isfinite(gB),gB,0.0)
   return f,gB
```

```
def _local_search(a0, end_time, seed):
  a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; L=_pow2(2*n-1)
  c=_fft_conv(a,L); best=a; best_sc=_score_c(c,n)
  rng=...
  sigma=0.12; last_best=now
  while now<end_time:
    cand=best.copy(); nblocks=1+int(rng.integers(0,2))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
       maxw=max(3,n//10)
       w=int(exp(rng.uniform(log2, log(maxw))))
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
       cand[s:s+w] *= exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
    cand=_normalize(cand); sc=_score_norm(cand,L)
    if sc < best_sc -1e-12: update; sigma=min(0.35,sigma*1.05)
    else: if now-last>5 sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.8); if stuck recenter
  return best,best_sc
```

if deadline <= t0 + 20:
    a = _param_sequence(n,0.5,0,1,1,0,0,0)
    return list
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

The method combines:
  * robust FFT scoring,
  * one-sided/two-tailed parametric starts,
  * low-resolution block-constrained L-BFGS optimization on a smooth
    log-sum-exp surrogate,
  * full-length multiplicative smoothed-subgradient / Adam-type search
    with exact-score acceptance and adaptive restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize = None


def _pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_nonnegative(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(v, 0.0)


def _normalize(a):
    a = _clean_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _fft_conv(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _score_c(c, n):
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def _score_norm(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_conv(a, L)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s)


def _softmax_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -80.0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _log_of_a(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    eps = max(1e-300, 1e-15 * mx)
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -60.0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    return np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)


def _soft_value_T(c, T):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0
    T = float(T)
    if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 1e-300:
        T = 1e-300
    z = (c - mx) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return mx
    return mx + T * float(np.log(s))


def _resample_sequence(a0, n):
    a0 = _clean_nonnegative(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a0.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size == n:
        return _normalize(a0)
    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    y0 = np.where(np.isfinite(y0), y0, -50.0)
    if a0.size == 1:
        y = np.full(n, float(y0[0]))
    else:
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a0.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.interp(x1, x0, y0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)
    return _softmax_y(y)


def _param_sequence(n, p, q, alpha, beta, w_right, w_uniform, slope):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = float(p)
    q = float(q)
    alpha = max(1e-6, float(alpha))
    beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
    p = min(1.2, max(0.0, p))
    q = min(1.2, max(0.0, q))

    v = np.power(i + alpha, -p)

    if w_uniform:
        v = v + float(w_uniform)

    if slope:
        if n > 1:
            t = i / float(n - 1)
        else:
            t = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
        v = v * np.exp(float(slope) * t)

    if w_right:
        r = np.power((n - 1 - i) + beta, -q)
        v = v + float(w_right) * r

    return _normalize(v)


def _random_param(rng):
    p = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.70))
    if rng.random() < 0.48:
        q = 0.0
        w_right = 0.0
        beta = 1.0
    else:
        q = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
        w_right = float(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(0.01), np.log(5.0))))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5))
    alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    w_uniform = 0.0
    if rng.random() < 0.30:
        w_uniform = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.4))
    slope = 0.0
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        slope = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
    return (p, q, alpha, beta, w_right, w_uniform, slope)


def _fallback_sequence(n=1000):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _add_candidate(starts, a, L=None):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    if a.size <= 0:
        return
    sc = _score_norm(a, L)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((float(sc), a))


def _make_block_arrays(n, B):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    B = int(min(max(1, B), n))
    sizes = np.full(B, n // B, dtype=np.int64)
    rem = n % B
    if rem:
        sizes[:rem] += 1
    offsets = np.empty(B, dtype=np.int64)
    offsets[0] = 0
    if B > 1:
        offsets[1:] = np.cumsum(sizes, dtype=np.int64)[:-1]
    for b in range(B - 1):
        sizes[b] = int(offsets[b + 1] - offsets[b])
    return B, sizes, offsets


def _block_sequence_from_y(yB, n, sizes):
    yB = np.asarray(yB, dtype=np.float64)
    sizes = np.asarray(sizes, dtype=np.int64)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    B = int(sizes.size)
    if B == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if int(sizes.sum()) != n:
        sizes, _ = _make_block_arrays(n, B)

    eB = _softmax_y(yB)
    mass = eB.astype(np.float64) * sizes.astype(np.float64)
    s = float(mass.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    vals = eB / s
    a = np.repeat(vals, sizes)
    if a.size != n:
        a = _normalize(_resample_sequence(a, n))
    return a


def _block_soft_grad(x, n, T, L, sizes, offsets):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(int(np.asarray(sizes).size))

    a = _block_sequence_from_y(x, n, sizes)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, n=L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    f = _soft_value_T(c, T)

    B = int(np.asarray(sizes).size)
    if c.size == 0 or B == 0:
        return f, np.zeros(B)

    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 1e-300:
        return f, np.zeros(B)

    T = float(T)
    if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 1e-300:
        T = 1e-300

    z = (c - mx) / T
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = e / s

    W = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), n=L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, n=L)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    if conv.size < need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:conv.size] = conv
        conv = tmp
    else:
        conv = conv[:need]

    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    h = conv[idx]
    diag_idx = 2 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    ga = h + a * np.take(w, diag_idx)
    ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gy = a * (ga - dot)
    gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)

    offsets = np.asarray(offsets, dtype=np.int64)
    if B == 1:
        gB = np.array([float(gy.sum())], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        gB = np.add.reduceat(gy, offsets)
        if gB.size != B:
            gB = np.array([float(np.sum(gy[offs:offs + sz])) for offs, sz in zip(offsets, sizes)], dtype=np.float64)

    gB = np.asarray(gB, dtype=np.float64)
    gB = np.where(np.isfinite(gB), gB, 0.0)
    if gB.size:
        gB = gB - float(gB.mean())
    return f, gB


def _optimize_blocks(a0, n, B, end_time, seed):
    a0 = _normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n < 2:
        sc = _score_norm(a0)
        return a0, sc

    best = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(a0)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best, np.inf

    if _sp_minimize is None or end_time - time.monotonic() < 15.0:
        return best, best_sc

    B, sizes, offsets = _make_block_arrays(n, B)
    y_full = _log_of_a(a0)
    yB = np.full(B, -30.0, dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(B):
        st = int(offsets[b])
        en = int(offsets[b] + sizes[b])
        seg = y_full[st:en]
        if seg.size:
            med = float(np.median(seg))
            if np.isfinite(med):
                yB[b] = med
    yB = yB - np.max(yB)
    yB = np.clip(yB, -80.0, 0.0)

    L = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    current_yB = yB.copy()
    current_a = best.copy()
    current_sc = best_sc
    best_yB = yB.copy()

    tau_rels = (0.25, 0.10, 0.03, 0.01)
    bounds = [(-80.0, 80.0)] * B

    for tau_rel in tau_rels:
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
            break
        cmax = current_sc / (2.0 * float(n)) if current_sc > 0.0 else 0.0
        T = max(1e-300, float(tau_rel) * float(cmax))

        try:
            res = _sp_minimize(
                _block_soft_grad,
                current_yB.copy(),
                args=(n, T, L, sizes, offsets),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 60,
                    "maxfun": 100,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "disp": 0,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        y_try = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        y_try = np.where(np.isfinite(y_try), y_try, 0.0)
        if y_try.size:
            y_try = y_try - np.max(y_try)
            y_try = np.clip(y_try, -80.0, 0.0)

        a_try = _block_sequence_from_y(y_try, n, sizes)
        sc_try = _score_norm(a_try, L)

        if np.isfinite(sc_try):
            if sc_try < best_sc:
                best = a_try.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc_try)
                best_yB = y_try.copy()
            if sc_try <= current_sc + 0.05:
                current_yB = y_try.copy()
                current_a = a_try.copy()
                current_sc = float(sc_try)
            else:
                current_yB = best_yB.copy()
                current_a = best.copy()
                current_sc = best_sc

    return best, best_sc


def _subgradient_refine(y, end_time, seed):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0), np.inf
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -40.0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)

    n = int(y.size)
    if n <= 1:
        a = _softmax_y(y)
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        return a, sc

    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lgrad = _pow2(3 * n - 2)

    a = _softmax_y(y)
    c = _fft_conv(a, Lscore)
    sc = _score_c(c, n)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a = _fallback_sequence(n)
        c = _fft_conv(a, Lscore)
        sc = _score_c(c, n)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(sc)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) & 0x7fffffff)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.99
    p1 = 1.0
    p2 = 1.0

    lr = 0.02
    lr_min = 1e-5
    lr_max = 0.25
    tau_rel = 0.25

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_best = t0
    last_print = t0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        now = time.monotonic()

        mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 1e-300:
            break

        T = max(1e-300, tau_rel * mx)
        z = (c - mx) / T
        z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(c.size)
        else:
            w = e / s
        soft_cur = mx + T * float(np.log(s))

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=Lgrad)
        W = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), n=Lgrad)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, n=Lgrad)
        need = 3 * n - 2
        if conv.size < need:
            tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
            tmp[:conv.size] = conv
            conv = tmp
        else:
            conv = conv[:need]

        idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        h = conv[idx]
        diag = 2 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        ga = h + a * np.take(w, diag)
        ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)

        dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        g = a * (ga - dot)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

        t += 1
        p1 *= b1
        p2 *= b2
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        mh = m / max(1e-16, 1.0 - p1)
        vh = v / max(1e-16, 1.0 - p2)
        step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
        step = np.clip(step, -0.25, 0.25)

        accepted = False
        last_soft = soft_cur
        for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            y_new = y - scale * step
            y_new = np.where(np.isfinite(y_new), y_new, 0.0)
            y_new = y_new - np.max(y_new)
            y_new = np.clip(y_new, -80.0, 0.0)

            a_new = _softmax_y(y_new)
            c_new = _fft_conv(a_new, Lscore)
            sc_new = _score_c(c_new, n)
            if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
                continue

            soft_new = _soft_value_T(c_new, T)

            if (sc_new < sc - 1e-12) or (
                soft_new < soft_cur - 1e-12 and sc_new <= sc + 1e-9
            ):
                y = y_new
                a = a_new
                c = c_new
                sc = float(sc_new)
                last_soft = float(soft_new)
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if sc < best_sc:
                best = a.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                last_best = now
                no_improve = 0
                lr = min(lr_max, lr * 1.02)
                tau_rel = max(0.005, tau_rel * 0.97)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.9)
            tau_rel = min(0.8, tau_rel * 1.1)

        if no_improve > 60 or now - last_best > 45.0:
            y = _log_of_a(best)
            y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -30.0)
            y = y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
            y = y - np.max(y)
            y = np.clip(y, -80.0, 0.0)

            a = _softmax_y(y)
            c = _fft_conv(a, Lscore)
            sc = _score_c(c, n)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                a = best.copy()
                c = _fft_conv(a, Lscore)
                sc = best_sc

            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            p1 = 1.0
            p2 = 1.0
            no_improve = 0
            last_best = now
            lr = 0.02
            tau_rel = 0.30

        if now - last_print >= 30.0:
            print(
                "subgrad n=%d score=%.8f best=%.8f tau_rel=%.4f lr=%.5f time=%.1f"
                % (n, float(sc), float(best_sc), float(tau_rel), float(lr), float(now - t0))
            )
            last_print = now

    return best, float(best_sc)


def _local_search(a0, end_time, seed):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, _score_norm(a)

    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _fft_conv(a, L)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_c(c, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return a, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) & 0x7fffffff)
    sigma = 0.12
    sigma_max = 0.35
    sigma_min = 0.02
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    t0 = last_best

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        now = time.monotonic()
        cand = best.copy()
        nblocks = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 2))

        for _ in range(nblocks):
            maxw = max(3, n // 10)
            w = int(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(2.0), np.log(float(maxw)))))
            w = max(1, min(w, n))
            s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
            cand[s:s + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))

        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_norm(cand, L)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_sc = float(sc)
            last_best = now
            sigma = min(sigma_max, sigma * 1.05)
        else:
            if now - last_best > 5.0:
                sigma = max(sigma_min, sigma * 0.8)
            if now - last_best > 25.0 and now - t0 > 30.0:
                cand2 = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n))
                cand2 = _normalize(cand2)
                sc2 = _score_norm(cand2, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best = cand2
                    best_sc = float(sc2)
                    last_best = now

    return best, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2395.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        b = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        b = 2395.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0.0:
        b = 2395.0
    if b > 2395.0:
        b = 2395.0
    if b < 60.0:
        b = max(60.0, b)
    deadline = t0 + b - 8.0

    if b > 1800.0:
        n_main = 10000
    elif b > 1000.0:
        n_main = 6000
    elif b > 400.0:
        n_main = 4000
    else:
        n_main = 2000

    if deadline <= t0 + 25.0:
        return [float(x) for x in _param_sequence(n_main, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]

    starts = []
    Lscore = _pow2(2 * n_main - 1)

    # Use any previously found construction, both exactly and resampled.
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
        if parent is not None:
            parr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if parr.ndim == 1 and parr.size >= 2:
                _add_candidate(starts, parr, None)
                if parr.size != n_main:
                    _add_candidate(starts, _resample_sequence(parr, n_main), Lscore)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Fixed family starts.
    for p in (0.45, 0.475, 0.5, 0.525, 0.55):
        for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            _add_candidate(
                starts,
                _param_sequence(n_main, p, 0.0, alpha, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
                Lscore,
            )

    for wr in (0.05, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0, 3.0):
        for q in (0.1, 0.3, 0.5):
            _add_candidate(
                starts,
                _param_sequence(n_main, 0.5, q, 1.0, 1.0, wr, 0.0, 0.0),
                Lscore,
            )

    for sym_p in (0.30, 0.38, 0.45):
        _add_candidate(
            starts,
            _param_sequence(n_main, sym_p, sym_p, 0.8, 0.8, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            Lscore,
        )

    # Random parametric starts.
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) & 0x7fffffff)
    n_random = 360 if b > 1200 else 140
    gen_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(25.0, 0.045 * b))

    for _ in range(n_random):
        if time.monotonic() >= gen_deadline:
            break
        p, q, alpha, beta, wr, wu, slope = _random_param(rng)
        a = _param_sequence(n_main, p, q, alpha, beta, wr, wu, slope)
        _add_candidate(starts, a, Lscore)

    if not starts:
        starts.append((2.0, _fallback_sequence(n_main)))

    starts.sort(key=_score_key)

    best_a = np.asarray(starts[0][1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_sc = float(starts[0][0])
    print("initial best candidate score=%.8f length=%d" % (best_sc, best_a.size))

    # Macro block polish of the best low-resolution starts.
    if _sp_minimize is not None and b > 250.0:
        macro_time_each = min(45.0, max(15.0, 0.025 * b))
        top_macro = starts[:3]
        for i, (sc, a) in enumerate(top_macro):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                break
            alen = int(a.size)
            if alen < 500 or alen > 15000:
                continue
            B = 64 if alen >= 4000 else 32
            end_i = min(deadline - 45.0, time.monotonic() + macro_time_each)
            try:
                am, scm = _optimize_blocks(a, alen, B, end_i, int(seed) + 101 * (i + 1))
            except Exception:
                am, scm = a, sc
            if np.isfinite(scm) and scm < best_sc + 0.25:
                _add_candidate(starts, am, None)
                print(
                    "macro start %d im score=%.8f length=%d"
                    % (i + 1, float(scm), int(am.size))
                )

        starts.sort(key=_score_key)
        best_a = np.asarray(starts[0][1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
        best_sc = float(starts[0][0])
        print("after macro best score=%.8f length=%d" % (best_sc, best_a.size))

    # Full-length refinement from the best starts.
    top = starts[:6]
    if top:
        fracs = [0.34, 0.24, 0.17, 0.12, 0.08, 0.05]
        fracs = fracs[: len(top)]
        fr_sum = float(sum(fracs))
        if fr_sum <= 0.0:
            fracs = [1.0 / float(len(top))] * len(top)
        else:
            fracs = [x / fr_sum * 0.90 for x in fracs]

        base = time.monotonic()
        alloc_budget = max(0.0, deadline - base)
        used = 0.0

        for i, (sc, a) in enumerate(top):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
                break
            frac = fracs[i]
            end_i = min(deadline - 15.0, base + (used + frac) * alloc_budget)
            used += frac
            if end_i <= time.monotonic() + 30.0:
                continue

            alen = int(a.size)
            if alen > 20000:
                continue

            y = _log_of_a(a)
            try:
                ar, cr = _subgradient_refine(y, end_i, int(seed) + 2017 * (i + 1))
            except Exception:
                ar, cr = a, sc

            ar = _normalize(np.asarray(ar, dtype=np.float64))
            if np.isfinite(cr) and ar.size > 0:
                # Re-evaluate to protect against rare numerical drift.
                cr = _score_norm(ar)
                if np.isfinite(cr) and cr < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(cr)
                    best_a = ar.copy()
                    print("full refine start %d new best score=%.8f length=%d" % (i + 1, best_sc, int(ar.size)))

    # Final continuation on the globally best sequence.
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 40.0:
        y = _log_of_a(best_a)
        sub_end = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.70 * remaining))
        try:
            ar, cr = _subgradient_refine(y, sub_end, int(seed) + 99999)
        except Exception:
            ar, cr = best_a, best_sc
        ar = _normalize(np.asarray(ar, dtype=np.float64))
        if ar.size > 0 and np.isfinite(cr):
            cr = _score_norm(ar)
            if np.isfinite(cr) and cr < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(cr)
                best_a = ar.copy()

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 20.0:
        try:
            ar, cr = _local_search(best_a, min(deadline, time.monotonic() + remaining), int(seed) + 123456)
        except Exception:
            ar, cr = best_a, best_sc
        ar = _normalize(np.asarray(ar, dtype=np.float64))
        if ar.size > 0 and np.isfinite(cr):
            cr = _score_norm(ar)
            if np.isfinite(cr) and cr < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(cr)
                best_a = ar.copy()

    best_a = _normalize(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64))
    if best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = _fallback_sequence(n_main)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    try:
        val = _score_norm(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            out = [float(x) for x in _fallback_sequence(n_main)]
        else:
            print("final internal score=%.8f length=%d" % (float(val), len(out)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
